Apply placeholder mapping\n            for k, v in mapping.items():\n                if k in new:\n                    new = new.replace(k, v)\n\n            # Convert the inspection date wherever it appears literally\n            if date_raw and date_raw in new:\n                new = new.replace(date_raw, date_fmt)\n\n            # Ensure risk tier text includes severity note everywhere it appears.\n            # Avoid double-annotating.\n            if tier_raw:\n                if tier_annotated in new:\n                    pass\n                else:\n                    # Some templates nest linesegarray not as direct child; remove descendants too.\n            for ls in list(p.findall(\".//hp:linesegarray\", NS)):\n                parent = _find_parent(root, ls)\n                if parent is not None:\n                    parent.remove(ls)\n                    removed_any = True\n            for ls in list(p.findall(\".//hp:lineSegArray\", NS)):\n                parent = _find_parent(root, ls)\n                if parent is not None:\n                    parent.remove(ls)\n                    removed_any = True\n\n            modified_paras += 1\n\n    # Sanity check: no placeholders left\n    leftovers = []\n    placeholder_re = re.compile(r\"\\{\\{[^}]+\\}\\}\")\n    for xp in sorted(xml_files):\n        with open(xp, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n            text = f.read()\n        m = placeholder_re.search(text)\n        if m:\n            leftovers.append((xp, m.group(0)))\n\n    if leftovers:\n        for xp, token in leftovers:\n            print(f\"ERROR: leftover placeholder {token} in {xp}\")\n        return 1\n\n    print(f\"OK: modified paragraphs: {total_modified}\")\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}
